## Finishes

- [Anodizing](https://www.fictiv.com/finishes/anodizing) - Anodizing grows the natural oxide layer on aluminum parts for wear and corrosion protection. Get cnc machined parts anodized with Fictiv.
- [Chem Film](https://www.fictiv.com/finishes/chem-film) - Chem Film, aka, Alodine™ or Chromate Conversion Coating, is a chemical treatment applied to for aesthetic reasons and to protect metals from corrosion.
- [Powder Coating Services](https://www.fictiv.com/finishes/powder-coating) - Powder coating is a finishing process for aluminum, steel, and stainless steel which results in a thicker, tough coating. Read more about powder coating here.
- [Zinc Plating](https://www.fictiv.com/finishes/zinc-plating) - Zinc plating, which is also known as galvanization, is applied to prevent the surface from oxidizing or corroding. Read more about its application & benefits.
- [Black Oxide](https://www.fictiv.com/finishes/black-oxide) - Black oxide is a conversion coating (like Chem film) that is used for steel and stainless steel. Get Fictiv's Black Oxide Coating Service.
- [Chem film or Alodine™](https://www.fictiv.com/finishes/alodine-chromate-conversion-coating) - Chem film or Alodine™ or chromate conversion coating is a chemical coating that protects aluminum from corrosion, which is thinner than anodizing. Read all about it here.
- [Vapor Smoothing](https://www.fictiv.com/finishes/vapor-smoothing) - Discover the power of vapor smoothing for your 3D prints. Achieve smooth and polished surfaces with this effective post-processing technique.
- [Vapor Polishing](https://www.fictiv.com/finishes/vapor-polishing) - Unlock the secret to flawless 3D prints with vapor polishing. Learn how this technique can give your prints a professional and polished look.
- [Tumbling](https://www.fictiv.com/finishes/tumbling) - Tumble finishing is used to clean, deburr, and slightly smooth smaller parts. Read more about the tumbling process and its advantages here.
- [Nickel Plating](https://www.fictiv.com/finishes/nickel-plating) - Nickel plating on metal provides corrosion and wear resistance, as well as aesthetics. Read about the benefits & application of nickel plating here.
- [Electroless Nickel Plating](https://www.fictiv.com/finishes/electroless-nickel-plating) - Electroless nickel plating deposits a nickel-phosphorus alloy onto the surface of a metal. Read about the benefits & applications of electroless nickel plating.
- [Media Blasting](https://www.fictiv.com/finishes/media-blasting) - Media blasting uses a pressurized jet of abrasive media to apply a matte, uniform finish to the surface of parts. Read more about media blasting here.
- [Heat Treatment](https://www.fictiv.com/finishes/heat-treatment) - Heat Treatment Finish: Enhance the strength and durability of steel and cast iron with hardening, case hardening, and tempering.
- [Hand Polishing](https://www.fictiv.com/finishes/hand-polishing) - Hand Polishing: Transform acrylic parts into smooth, glossy finishes, perfect for enhanced cosmetic appearance with attention to detail.
- [Passivation](https://www.fictiv.com/finishes/passivation) - Passivation is a chemical reaction that causes a material to be less affected by corrosion or other environmental factors. Read more about passivation here.
- [Electropolishing](https://www.fictiv.com/finishes/electropolishing) - Electropolishing is used to improve the finish of a part by removing material to level its surface. Read more about its benefits & applications here.

- [Calculators](https://www.fictiv.com/specifications/calculators) - Quick calculation tools for common manufacturing tasks

- [Surface Finishes](https://www.fictiv.com/specifications/surface-finishes) - Ra reference, anodizing, plating, powder coat, ENP, passivation, and 8+ more finishes.
- [Injection Molding](https://www.fictiv.com/specifications/injection-molding) - Wall thickness, draft angles, ribs, gates, shrinkage, SRI finishes, and cost optimization.

- [Sheet Metal](https://www.fictiv.com/specifications/sheet-metal)
